- **New chengyu 画虎類犬:** created `chengyu/画虎類犬.md` (aliases include the 狗 forms and the full 畫虎不成反類狗; origin 後漢書 卷24, Ma Yuan's 誡兄子嚴敦書, checked on zh.Wikisource, where the original reads 狗; 注音 ㄏ⺢ㄎㄏㄛㄌㄨㄧ·ㄎ⼔ㄋ, dotted at 類|犬). Back-linked from 画, 虎, 類 and 犬; added to `chengyu/Misc. Chengyu.md` (size 159). The headword uses 犬 because 狗 is a 名専字.

- **New words 枯木, 生花 and chengyu 枯木生花:** created `words/枯木.md` (注音 ㄎㄛ·ㄇㄛㄎ, dotted because ㄎㄛㄇㄛㄎ also splits ㄎㄛㄇ + ㄛㄎ) and `words/生花.md` (注音 ㄙㄚㄫㄏ⺢; 花 stays bound, stand-in 草花), both on Plants; `chengyu/枯木生花.md` (origin 五燈會元 卷13, Dongshan Liangjie to Shushan, checked on zh.Wikisource with 指月錄 卷18; Wiktionary confirms 生花 as a literary verb 'to bloom'). Back-links added on 枯, 木, 生 and 花 under Words and Chengyu; added to `chengyu/Misc. Chengyu.md` (size 158) and `lexipedia/Plants.md`.

- **New chengyu 芒刺在背 (thorns on one's back):** created `chengyu/芒刺在背.md` (origin 漢書; alias 如芒在背; 注音 ㄇ⼘ㄫㄑㄧㄎㄐㄚㄧㄅㄛㄧ, dotless as in ㄉㄚㄧㄅ⼘ㄨ). Source checked on zh.Wikisource, 漢書 卷六十八 (霍光金日磾傳): 若有芒刺在背 is there, in Emperor Xuan's ride with Huo Guang; that text is only marked partly proofread, which the page says. Characters checked first (芒, 刺, 在, 背): all have pages with consistent readings. Back-linked from the four characters and added to `chengyu/Misc. Chengyu.md` (size 154).

- **New chengyu 膠柱鼓瑟 (gluing the bridges and playing the se):** created `chengyu/膠柱鼓瑟.md` (origin 史記; aliases 胶柱鼓瑟 and 膠柱而鼓瑟; 注音 ㄍ⼄ㄨㄐㄨㄍㄛㄙㄜㄊ, dotless). Source checked on zh.Wikisource, 史記 卷081 (廉頗藺相如列傳): Lin Xiangru's line 若膠柱而鼓瑟耳 is there; the four-character form without 而 is later. Characters checked first: 膠, 柱, 鼓 and 瑟 all have pages and consistent readings; one oddity found and left for the user (瑟's Japanese field lists SHICHI beside SHITSU). Back-linked from the four characters and added to `chengyu/Misc. Chengyu.md` (size 153). The outcome of the Changping campaign and the description of the se are from general knowledge and flagged on the page.

- **New chengyu 一気化三清 (one breath becomes the Three Pure Ones):** created `chengyu/一気化三清.md` (origin 封神演義; aliases 一氣化三清, 一气化三清, 一炁化三清, 一無化三清; 注音 ㄧㄊㄎㄧㄜㄏ⺢ㄙㄚㄇㄑㄧㄫ, dotless). Source checked on zh.Wikisource: the form is in 封神演義 chapter 78 (the closing line of Laozi's poem), and chapter 77, titled 老子一炁化三清, has the transformation itself; both quotations were found in the text. The pre-novel Daoist background and the authorship note are from general knowledge and flagged as such on the page. Back-linked from 一, 気, 化, 三 and 清, added to `chengyu/Misc. Chengyu.md` (size 152). lint_chengyu, lint_chengyu_groups and lint_characters pass.

- **玉皇, 大帝 and 天公 coined:** "Jade Emperor" (固有名詞; 注音 ⼄ㄎㄏ⺢ㄫ, `'yoghwang`, 욕황), "the Great; title of a Daoist deity" (名詞; ㄉㄚㄧㄊㄝㄧ, `daitei`, 대테) and "God; Jade Emperor; the heavens, weather" (名詞; ㄊㄝㄋㄍㄛㄫ, `tengong`, 턴공). Senses from en.Wiktionary and zh.Wikipedia and ja.Wikipedia (the Jade Emperor's titles, the Four Sovereigns; 大帝 for deities and monarchs; 天公不作美); the full 玉皇大帝 is compositional (玉皇 + 大帝), so no four-character entry. Added to `## Words` on 玉, 皇, 大, 帝, 天 and 公 and to lexipedia/Religion; stamped. Authority: user request 2026-11-08.

- **欲界, 色界 and 無色界 coined:** "desire realm; Kāmadhātu" (注音 ⼄ㄎㄍ⼶, `'yoggye`, 욕겨), "form realm; Rūpadhātu" (ㄙㄧㄎㄍ⼶, `siggye`, 식겨) and "formless realm; Ārūpyadhātu" (ㄇㄜㄙㄧㄎㄍ⼶, `mǝsiggye`, 므식겨), the three realms of 三界 (the three words now linked from its Notes). Sense from zh.Wikipedia and ja.Wikipedia (Abhidharmakośa account: the six desire heavens, the four meditation heavens, the four formless stages); readings: Japanese よくかい / しきかい / むしきかい, Korean 욕계 / 색계 / 무색계, Vietnamese dục giới / sắc giới / vô sắc giới (Mandarin and Cantonese compositional-attested). Added to `## Words` on 欲, 色, 界 and 無 and to lexipedia/Religion; stamped. Authority: user request 2026-11-08.

- **三界 coined (words/三界.md):** "three realms; Trailokya" (名詞); 三 + 界; 注音 ㄙㄚㄇㄍ⼶, 羅馬字 samgye, 諺文 삼겨; Wiktionary (trailokya), ja.Wikipedia and zh.Wikipedia for the three realms (欲界, 色界, 無色界), their ranking, the Daoist 三界二十八天 and 三世; Japanese さんがい, Korean 삼계, Vietnamese tam giới. Added to `## Words` on 三 and 界 and to lexipedia/Religion (Buddhist & Cosmological Concepts); stamped. Authority: user request 2026-11-08.

- **霊宝 coined (words/霊宝.md):** "numinous treasure; Lingbao" (名詞); 霊 + 宝, the shinjitai spelling of 靈寶 (aliases 靈寶, 灵宝); 注音 ㄌㄝㄫㄅㄚㄨ, 羅馬字 lengbau, 諺文 렁밧. Sense from en.Wikipedia (the Lingbao school) and ja.Wikipedia (れいほう, a temple's sacred treasure); the Daoist deity 靈寶天尊 composed as 霊宝 + 天尊, so no four-character entry. Added to `## Words` on 霊 and 宝 and to lexipedia/Religion; stamped. Authority: user request 2026-11-08.

- **元始 made an alias of 原始:** by the project owner's decision (Wiktionary lists 元始 as an alternative form of 原始 in Japanese, as in 元始に神天地を創造たまへり, Genesis 1:1; in Chinese it is the "Primordial" of 元始天尊). `words/原始.md` gained `aliases: 元始` and a sentence in its Notes. Authority: 武明帥.

- **天尊 coined (words/天尊.md):** "Heavenly Venerable; Honoured One; high deity" (名詞); 天 + 尊; 注音 ㄊㄝㄋㄐㄛㄋ, 羅馬字 tenjon, 諺文 턴존; Mandarin and Cantonese from en.Wiktionary (tiānzūn, tin1 zyun1), Japanese てんそん, Korean 천존 and Vietnamese thiên tôn compositional. Three uses recorded in the Notes (Buddhist, early rendering of Bhagavān; Daoist, the Three Pure Ones; Church of the East, God the Father), with its Zhang Hua origin and its relation to 天主, 神 and 佛. Added to `## Words` on 天 and 尊 and to lexipedia/Religion (Spirits & Souls); stamped. Authority: user request 2026-11-08.

- **鰭好 renamed 耆好; 嗜 moved from 鰭 to 耆; 耆 stays a 名専字:** by the project owner's decision (耆 is the original form of 嗜, so 耆好 is the natural spelling of 嗜好). `words/鰭好.md` is now `words/耆好.md` (characters 耆 + 好; aliases 嗜好 and 鰭好; a 代用字 note: 耆, a 名専字, stands in for 嗜); 耆 (char) keeps `stand_in: 名専字`, grade 名 and `danayo_id` 8827 (a first attempt that made 耆好 its stand-in and re-graded it 先進 was wrong, since 耆好 does not mean what 耆 means, and was reverted the same day), lists 耆好 beside the 名専字 line under `## Words`, and takes `aliases: 嗜` with its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 13, SKIP-1-3-10 and Radical 030; 嗜's rank 1740 is lower than 耆's 1634, so `mc_id` stays 1634). 鰭 loses the alias 嗜 and its 鰭好 Words entry. - **耆 created (characters/耆.md):** by the project owner's decision ("worth creating"); a classical character, "aged; a man of sixty" (also read shì as the original of 嗜, zhǐ "to cause"), 老 + 旨, 10 strokes, SKIP 2-6-4 (EDRDG), `mc_id` 1634 (Classical Chinese rank on `CC 1000`), 注音 ㄍㄧㄜ (`giǝ`, 긔, the slot of 鰭), grade 名, `danayo_id` 8827, `stand_in: 名専字`. - **𧶠 de-linked on 贖:** the red link `[[𧶠]]` (the original of 鬻 "to sell", phonetic of 贖, 続, 読; derived sound ⼄ㄎ) is plain text; no candidate fits sound and meaning. Authority: 武明帥.

- **雔 de-linked on 讐:** the red link `[[雔]]` ("a pair of birds", the phonetic of 讐; derived sound ㄙㄨ) is plain text; no candidate fits sound and meaning. Authority: 武明帥.

- **灞 made an alias of 覇, and 覇's `## Aliases` section built:** by the project owner's decision (a river-name character, 氵 + 霸, used only in 灞河; kept on the identical sound ㄅㄚ). 覇 gained `aliases: 灞` and its first `## Aliases` section for 霸, 灞, 䨣, 䩗, 䩻 and 𧈉 (redirects on Stroke 13, 17, 19, 21 and 24, SKIP-2-8-13, SKIP-1-3-21, SKIP-2-8-9, SKIP-1-9-4, SKIP-1-9-10 and SKIP-2-6-13, Radicals 085, 141, 173 and 177; the SKIP numbers of 䨣, 䩗, 䩻 and 𧈉 are computed). `mc_id` unchanged (712, the rank of 霸). The Derived Characters entry for 灞 (and the section, now empty) removed. 覊 and 羈 deferred. Authority: 武明帥.

- **絝 made an alias of 袴:** by the project owner's decision, on the identical sound (ㄎㄛ, MC `kʰ` + `uo`) and meaning ("pants"; Wiktionary: 絝 is an alternative form of 褲). 袴 (char) gained `aliases: 絝` and its first `## Aliases` section for 褲 and 絝 (redirects on Stroke 12 and 15, SKIP-1-5-10 and SKIP-1-6-6, Radicals 120 and 145). `mc_id` unchanged (4149). The 絝 link on 袴 now says "this page's alias". Authority: 武明帥.

- **巤 made an alias of 鬣, and 鬣 the derived-character hub for 巤:** by the project owner's decision, on the identical sound (ㄌㄛㄆ, MC `l` + `iᴇp`) and meaning (Wiktionary: 巤 is an alternative form of 鬣, "mane"). 鬣 (char) gained `aliases: 巤`, its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 15, SKIP-2-3-12 and Radical 047) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (獵, 蠟, 臘). `mc_id` unchanged (3632). The 巤 notes on 蠟, 鬣, 臘 and 獵 now point to 鬣; on 獵 the earlier `[[鼠|巤]]` link and its claim that 巤 is "an ancient variant of 鼠" (unsupported in the vault) are replaced. Authority: 武明帥.

- **蒦 made an alias of 獲, and 獲 the derived-character hub for 蒦:** by the project owner's decision, on the identical sound (ㄏ⺢ㄎ, MC `ɣ` + `wɑk`) and sense ("to catch"; 蒦 is the original of 獲). 獲 gained `aliases: 蒦`, its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 13, SKIP-2-3-10 and Radical 140) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (穫, 蠖, 護). `mc_id` unchanged (631). The 蒦 notes on 蠖, 護, 穫 and 獲 now point to 獲 ("no vault page" removed). Authority: 武明帥.

- **隡 de-linked on 薩:** the red link `[[隡]]` (a rare CNS 11643 form, 阝 + 產) is plain text; not aliased, as nothing needs it. Authority: 武明帥.

- **瞢 made an alias of 夢, and 夢 the derived-character hub for 瞢:** by the project owner's decision, on the identical sound (ㄇㄨㄫ, MC `m` + `ɨuŋ`) and because Wiktionary records 瞢 as a variant of 夢 (and of 盲) and it is 夢's Shuowen phonetic. 夢 (char) gained `aliases: 瞢` and its first `## Aliases` section for 梦, 瞢 and 㒱 (redirects on Stroke 05, 11 and 16, SKIP-2-8-3, SKIP-2-9-7 and SKIP-2-2-3, Radicals 011, 075 and 109; 㒱's SKIP computed) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (薨). `mc_id` unchanged (1041). The 瞢 links on 夢 and 薨 now point to 夢. Authority: 武明帥.

- **茻 made an alias of 莽:** by the project owner's decision, on the identical sound (ㄇㄚㄫ, MC `m` + `ɑŋ`) and the same sense (thick grass; 莽 is 犬 in 茻). 莽 gained `aliases: 茻` and its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 12, SKIP-2-6-6 and Radical 140; SKIP computed). `mc_id` unchanged (624). No other vault character builds on 茻, so no Derived Characters section. The 茻 link on 莽 is now "this page's alias", and the List of 会意 entry for 寒 links 茻 to 莽 with the "no vault page" note removed. Authority: 武明帥.

- **厷 made an alias of 宏, and 宏 the derived-character hub for 厷:** by the project owner's decision, because Wiktionary records 厷 (the original form of 肱) as a variant of 宏 and its derived sound is close (MC 古弘切, `k` + `wəŋ`, against 宏's `ɣ` + `ɣwɛŋ`). 宏 gained `aliases: 厷`, its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 04, SKIP-3-2-2 and Radical 028) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (紘, 雄). `mc_id` unchanged (2160). The 厷 mentions on 紘, 宏 and 雄 now point to 宏. Authority: 武明帥.

- **爯 made an alias of 称:** by the project owner's decision, on the identical sound (ㄑㄧㄫ, MC `t͡ɕʰ` + `ɨŋ`) and because 爯 is the ancient form of 稱 and 称's phonetic. 称 gained `aliases: 爯` and its first `## Aliases` section for 稱, 秤 and 爯 (redirects on Stroke 09, 10 and 14, SKIP-1-5-9, SKIP-1-5-5 and the new redirect-only leaf SKIP-2-1-8, Radicals 087 and 115; 秤 already had Jinmeiyō). `mc_id` unchanged (274, the rank of 稱). New leaf `SKIP-2-1-8` indexed with `ø` in SKIP-2-1. The 爯 note on 称 says "this page's alias". Authority: 武明帥.

- **禱 de-linked on 祷:** the red link `[[禱]]` pointed at the page's own alias; it is plain text. Authority: 武明帥.

- **欸 made an alias of 唉:** by the project owner's decision, on the identical sound (ㄚㄧ, MC `ʔ` + `ʌi`) and meaning (an interjection), and Wiktionary's "alternative form of 唉". 唉 (char) gained `aliases: 欸` and its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 11, SKIP-1-7-4 and Radical 076). `mc_id` unchanged (7655). On 矣 (char) the note on 欸 is now plain (with the history sentence about a leaked `english` field deleted) and the Derived Characters entry for 欸 is replaced by 唉. Authority: 武明帥.

- **夲 made an alias of 本:** by the project owner's decision, because Wiktionary records 夲 as a variant of 本 (Chinese, Vietnamese) and an alternative form of 本 (Japanese), with identical sound and meaning for that reading; its own rare reading (tāo, "to advance quickly") is the phonetic 皐 cites. 本 (char) gained `aliases: 夲`, its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 05, SKIP-2-3-2 and Radical 037) and a line about 皐 under Derived Characters. `mc_id` unchanged (302). The 夲 link on 皐 now points to 本. Authority: 武明帥.

- **癹 de-linked on 発 (char):** the red link `[[癹]]` (the phonetic of 発, "to level grass with the feet") is plain text; not aliased, as nothing needs it. Authority: 武明帥.

- **嵒 made an alias of 岩, and 岩 the derived-character hub for 嵒:** by the project owner's decision, on the identical sound (ㄚㄇ) and meaning (嵒 is a variant of 岩 and 巖) and because it is the phonetic of 癌. 岩 gained `aliases: 嵒`, 嵒 in its `## Aliases` section beside 巖 and 巌 (redirects on Stroke 12, SKIP-2-3-9 and Radical 046) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (癌). `mc_id` unchanged (2209, 巖's rank). The 嵒 link on 癌 now points to 岩. Authority: 武明帥.

- **玨 de-linked on 瑟:** the red link `[[玨]]` ("two jades", mentioned only to say it is unrelated to 珡) is plain text; no candidate fits sound and meaning. Authority: 武明帥.

- **𩕨 de-linked on 瀕:** the red link `[[𩕨]]` (a zh.Wiktionary-only variant, Extension B, no definition) is plain text; not aliased, for lack of corroboration. Authority: 武明帥.

- **𤅍 made an alias of 漕 (fiat):** by the project owner's decision, although the only evidence is en.Wiktionary's `alt=𤅍` on 漕 (no entry of its own, no zh.Wiktionary corroboration); it would share 漕's sound, ㄐㄚㄨ. 漕 gained `aliases: 𤅍` and its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 23, SKIP-1-3-20 and Radical 085; SKIP computed). `mc_id` unchanged (2598). The note on 漕 now says the page keeps 𤅍 as its alias. Authority: 武明帥.

- **冓 made an alias of 構, and 構 the derived-character hub for 冓:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (冓 would read ㄍㄛㄨ like 構, 溝 and 購, MC `k` + `əu`) and because 構 keeps 冓's "interlocked timbers" sense. 構 gained `aliases: 冓`, its first `## Aliases` section (构 and 冓; redirects on Stroke 08 and 10, SKIP-1-4-4 and SKIP-2-5-5, Radicals 075 and 013) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (溝, 購, 講). `mc_id` unchanged (2327, 構's own rank). The 冓 links and "no vault page" notes on 溝, 購, 講, 構 and the List of 会意 (再) now point to 構. SKIP-2-5-5: the leftover `### Redirects` section (including the wrong `冓 --> 勾`) removed, and its four forbidden lines shortened to `- ¡X! - 違法字`. Authority: 武明帥.

- **𠯑 made an alias of 括, and 括 the derived-character hub for 𠯑:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (𠯑 would read ㄍ⺢ㄊ like 括 and 刮, MC `k` + `uɑt`) and the direct etymology 手 + 𠯑. 括 gained `aliases: 𠯑` (the key was blank), its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 07, SKIP-2-4-3 and Radical 030; SKIP computed) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (活, 刮, 話). `mc_id` unchanged (1919). The 𠯑 links and "no vault page" notes on 活, 刮, 話 and 舌 now point to 括. Authority: 武明帥.

- **汮 de-linked on 洵:** the red link `[[汮]]` (an ancient river name; derived sound ㄍ⼜ㄋ, like 均) is plain text; no candidate fits sound and meaning. Authority: 武明帥.

- **㐆 made an alias of 隠, and 隠 the derived-character hub for 㐆:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (㐆 would read ㄜㄋ like 隠 and 殷, MC `ʔ` + `ɨn`) and because Wiktionary records 㐆 as an alternative form of 隱. 隠 gained `aliases: 㐆`, its first `## Aliases` section for 隱, 㐆 and 乚 (redirects on Stroke 01, 05 and 16, SKIP-1-3-14, SKIP-3-1-4 and SKIP-4-1-4, Radicals 004, 005 and 170; 㐆's SKIP computed) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (殷). `mc_id` unchanged (687, the rank of 隱). The 㐆 links on 殷 and the List of 会意 (the "no vault page" note removed) now point to 隠. Authority: 武明帥.

- **嗮 made a forbidden character, 澌 plain text; 晒 cleaned:** 嗮 (口 + 晒, a Cantonese-only aspect marker, "completely, all") added to 文法 - 98違法字 as 方言 (Chinese 135 → 136, total 441 → 442) with Stroke 13, SKIP-1-3-10 and Radical 030 lines `- ¡嗮! - 違法字`. On 晒 (char) the links to 嗮 and 澌 are plain text (嗮 with its forbidden note, 澌 "to drain dry"), and the unsupported claim that 嗮 is "etymologically tied to 澌" is deleted. Authority: 武明帥.

- **樊 made an alias of 藩, and 藩 the derived-character hub for 樊:** by the project owner's decision, on meaning (both are "fence, hedge") and the same Middle Chinese slot (附袁切, `v` + `ʉɐn`; the vault's characters in that slot read ㄆㄛㄋ, ㄆㄛㄇ or ㄈㄛㄇ, so the derived sound is a range). 藩 gained `aliases: 樊`, its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 15, SKIP-2-12-3 and Radical 075) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (攀, 礬). `mc_id` is now 1336, 樊's Classical Chinese rank (藩 was 1786). The 樊 links on 攀 and 礬 now point to 藩 (Korean Name ㅂ already did). Authority: 武明帥.

- **敝 made an alias of 蔽, and 蔽 the derived-character hub for 敝:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (敝 is 毗祭切 like 蔽 and 幣, ㄆㄝ) and because Wiktionary records 敝 as an alternative form of 蔽 and 弊; 蔽 chosen over 弊, whose sound (ㄆㄝㄧ) differs. 蔽 gained `aliases: 敝`, its first `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 11, SKIP-1-8-4 and Radical 066) and `## Derived Characters` with a short explanation (幣, 弊, 撇, 瞥). `mc_id` unchanged (1127). The 敝 links and "pageless" notes on 撇, 瞥, 弊 and 幣 now point to 蔽. Authority: 武明帥.

- **毄 made an alias of 撃, and 撃 the derived-character hub for 毄:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (毄 would read ㄍㄝㄎ like 撃, MC `k` + `ek`) and the same sense ("strike"; 擊 is 毄 with 手 added). 撃 (char) gained `aliases: 毄` and its first `## Aliases` section (擊, 毄 and 击; redirects on Stroke 05, 14 and 17, SKIP-2-13-4, SKIP-1-10-4 and SKIP-2-2-3, Radicals 017, 064 and 079; 击's SKIP computed) and `## Derived Characters` (繋). `mc_id` unchanged (259, the rank of 擊). The 毄 link on 繋 now points to 撃. Authority: 武明帥.

- **豦 made an alias of 拠, and 拠 the derived-character hub for 豦:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (豦 would read ㄍ⼄ like 拠 and 遽, MC `ɡ` + `ɨʌ`) and because it is the phonetic of 拠, 遽 and 劇; 劇's sound differs (ㄍㄝㄎ), so 拠 was chosen over it, and 拠 over 遽 as the more used (`mc_id` 975). 拠 gained `aliases: 豦` and its first `## Aliases` section (據, 豦 and 据; redirects on Stroke 11, 13 and 16, SKIP-1-3-13, SKIP-3-4-9 and SKIP-1-3-8, Radicals 064 and 152) and `## Derived Characters` (遽, 劇). `mc_id` unchanged (975, the rank of 據). The `[[豦]]` links on 拠, 遽 and 劇 now point to 拠. Authority: 武明帥.

- **榦 made an alias of 幹:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (榦 reads ㄍㄚㄋ like 幹, MC `k` + `ɑn`) and because 榦 is the original form of 幹 (倝 + 木, later 木 replaced by 干). 幹 gained `aliases: 榦` and 榦 in its `## Aliases` section beside 倝 (redirects on Stroke 14, SKIP-1-8-6 and Radical 075, in its +10 group); 幹 was already the hub for 倝. `mc_id` unchanged (1816). The `[[榦]]` link on 戟 now points to 幹. Authority: 武明帥.

- **貇 made an alias of 懇, and 懇 the derived-character hub for 貇:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (貇 would read ㄎㄚㄋ like 懇 and 墾, MC `kʰ` + `ən`) and because Wiktionary records 貇 as a variant of 懇 and it is the phonetic of 懇 and 墾. 懇 gained `aliases: 貇`, 貇 in its `## Aliases` section beside 恳 (redirects on Stroke 13, SKIP-1-7-6 and Radical 153) and `## Derived Characters` (墾). `mc_id` unchanged (4033). The note on 墾 now links 貇 to 懇. Authority: 武明帥.

- **囟 de-linked on 思:** the red link `[[囟]]` (fontanelle; phonetic of 思; derived sound ㄙㄧㄋ against 思's ㄙㄚ) is plain text; no alias candidate fits sound and meaning. Authority: 武明帥.

- **歁 de-linked on 嵌:** the red link `[[歁]]` (abbreviated phonetic of 嵌; "not satiated") is plain text; same derived sound as 嵌 (ㄎㄚㄇ) but no variant or meaning link. Authority: 武明帥.

- **屆 de-linked on 届 (char):** the red link `[[屆]]` pointed at the page's own alias; it is plain text ("traditional 屆, this page's alias"). Authority: 武明帥.

- **萈 de-linked on 寛:** the red link `[[萈]]` (obsolete "goat", the phonetic of 寛; derived sound ㄏ⺢ㄋ against 寛's ㄎ⺢ㄋ) is now plain text; no alias candidate fits sound and meaning. Authority: 武明帥.

- **痒 made an alias of 羊, and 掻羊 coined for "itch":** by the project owner's decision, 痒 (疒 + phonetic 羊, "itchy") would only occur in compounds and ⼘ㄫ is already crowded, so it is an alias of 羊 and gets no page. 羊 gained `aliases: 痒` and its first `## Aliases` section (𦍌, 佯, 痒; redirects on Stroke 06, 08 and 11, SKIP-2-3-3, SKIP-1-2-6 and SKIP-3-5-6, Radicals 123, 009 and 104; 𦍌's SKIP computed); `mc_id` unchanged (681). The `[[痒]]` links on Old HSK 4 and Korean Name ㅇ now point to 羊, and the wrong redirect `痒 --> 瘍` on Hyōgai is now `痒 --> 羊`; the "removed 痒" history clause on 瘍 is deleted. New word `words/掻羊.md` (名詞, "itch"; 掻 + 羊; ㄙㄚㄨ⼘ㄫ, `sau'yang`, 삿양; readings from en.Wiktionary 搔癢, 掻痒 and 搔痒, Vietnamese omitted; aliases 搔羊, 搔癢, 搔痒, 掻痒, 掻癢, 瘙癢, 瘙痒), added to `## Words` on 掻 and 羊 and to lexipedia/Sensation. Authority: 武明帥.

- **粲 made an alias of 燦:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (粲 would read ㄑㄚㄋ like 燦, MC `t͡sʰ` + `ɑn`) and because Wiktionary says they are the same word (燦 is 粲 + 火). 燦 gained `aliases: 粲` and its first `## Aliases` section (灿 and 粲; redirects on Stroke 07 and 13, SKIP-1-4-3 and SKIP-2-7-6, Radicals 086 and 119; 灿's SKIP computed). `mc_id` is now 3105, 粲's Classical Chinese rank (燦 itself is not on the lists). No other vault character is built on 粲, so no Derived Characters section. The `[[粲]]` links on 燦 and Korean Name ㅊ now point to 燦. Authority: 武明帥.

- **Radical 085, SKIP-1-3-15 and Stroke 18 brought to the forbidden-line standard:** Radical 085 migrated to the in-group format (29 aliases and 10 forbidden entries now under their `+N Strokes` groups, the bottom sections removed, numbered entries unchanged); the forbidden lines on all three pages shortened to `- ¡X! - 違法字` (Stroke 18's four bare entries 鯎 鯏 鯑 𩸽 gained the marker). New tool `AIOS/scripts/radical_migrate.py <n>` does the Radical migration. Authority: 武明帥.

- **潢 made an alias of 黄, and 黄's `## Aliases` section built:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (潢 would read ㄏ⺢ㄫ like 黄, MC `ɣ` + `wɑŋ`) and because 黄 is its phonetic source; 黄 (char) was already the hub (広, 横). 黄 gained `aliases: 潢` and its first `## Aliases` section for 黃, 簧 and 潢 (redirects on Stroke 12, 14 and 17, SKIP-2-4-8, SKIP-2-6-12 and SKIP-1-3-12, Radicals 085, 118 and 201; 黃's SKIP computed). `mc_id` unchanged (228, the rank of 黃). The `[[潢]]` links on 洸 and Korean Name ㅎ now point to 黄. Authority: 武明帥.

- **挪 made an alias of 那, with 那 as the hub:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (挪 would read ㄋㄚ, MC `n` + `ɑ`, like 那 and 梛) and because Wiktionary lists 那 as an obsolete alternative form of 挪. 那 (char) gained `aliases: 挪` and its first `## Aliases` section (哪 and 挪; redirects on Stroke 10, SKIP-1-3-7 and Radicals 030 and 064; 10 strokes counted the Japanese way); its existing Derived Characters (梛) are unchanged. `mc_id` unchanged (3626). The `[[挪]]` links on 梛 and Korean Name ㄴ now point to 那. Authority: 武明帥.

- **恬 made an alias of 甜, and 甜 the derived-character hub for 恬:** by the project owner's decision, on the derived Dan'a'yo sound (恬 and 甜 are both 徒兼切, MC `d` + `em`, so 恬 would read ㄉㄧㄇ like 甜) and because 甜 is 恬's phonetic source; the meanings differ ("sweet" and "tranquil"). 甜 gained `aliases: 恬`, a `## Aliases` section (redirects on Stroke 09, SKIP-1-3-6 and Radical 061) and `## Derived Characters` (憩). `mc_id` is now 1765, 恬's Classical Chinese rank (甜 was 7105). The `[[恬]]` links on 憩 and Korean Name ㄴ now point to 甜. Authority: 武明帥.

- **臾 made an alias of 庾, and 庾 the derived-character hub for 臾:** by the project owner's decision, on sound (Korean 유; 臾 is the phonetic of 庾), although 臾 is not a form of 庾. 庾 gained `aliases: 臾`, a `## Aliases` section (斞 and 臾; redirects on Stroke 08 and 12, SKIP-4-9-4 and SKIP-1-8-4, Radicals 068 and 134; 斞's SKIP computed) and `## Derived Characters` (貴). `mc_id` is now 2028, 臾's Classical Chinese rank. The `[[臾]]` links on 庾, 貴 and Korean Name ㅇ now point to 庾. Two history notes on 庾 deleted (the "`aliases: 臾` was removed" remark and the "mc_id off-by-one bug" note). Authority: 武明帥.

- **熒 made an alias of 蛍, and 蛍 the derived-character hub for 熒:** by the project owner's decision, on sound and meaning (熒 and the phonetic of 蛍 share OC \*ɡʷeːŋ and Korean 형; 蛍 is the luminous insect). 蛍 (char) gained `aliases: 熒`, a `## Aliases` section (螢, 萤, 熒; redirects on Stroke 11, 14 and 16, SKIP-2-8-8, SKIP-2-3-8 and SKIP-2-8-6, Radicals 086, 140 and 142; 萤's SKIP computed) and `## Derived Characters` (営, 労, 栄). `mc_id` is now 1598, 熒's Classical Chinese rank. The 熒 links on 営, 労, 栄, the List of 会意 and Korean Name ㅎ now point to 蛍; 栄 is not the parent, although Wiktionary treats 榮 and 熒 as one word, because the sound differs. Authority: 武明帥.

- **奐 made an alias of 煥, and 煥 the derived-character hub for 奐:** by the project owner's decision (奐 is the phonetic and the original sense, "brilliant", of 煥; Wiktionary also notes it was used for 換). 煥 gained `aliases: 奐`, a `## Aliases` section (烉, 焕, 奐; redirects on Stroke 09, 10 and 11, SKIP-2-2-7, SKIP-1-4-6 and SKIP-1-4-7, Radicals 037 and 086; 烉 and 焕 SKIP computed) and `## Derived Characters` (喚, 換). `mc_id` is now 2489, 奐's Classical Chinese rank (煥 itself is 3571). The `[[奐]]` red links on 喚, 換 and Korean Name ㅎ now point to 煥. No new character page. Authority: 武明帥.

- **剔 made an alias of 剃, and 剃 given its `## Aliases` section:** by the project owner's decision (Wiktionary records 剔 as an alternative form of 剃, "to shave", though in Chinese it is also a character of its own, "to pick out"). 剃 (char) gained `aliases: 剔` and a `## Aliases` section for all four aliases (鬀, 鬄, 薙, 剔), with redirects on Stroke 10, 16, 17 and 18, SKIP-1-8-2, SKIP-2-10-7, SKIP-2-10-8, SKIP-2-3-13 and Radicals 018, 140 and 190 (薙 already had Jinmeiyō and Stroke 16 lines); `[[剔]]` on Old HSK 6 and Korean Name ㅊ now `[[剃 (char)|剔]]`, the 剃 note says "this page's alias", and 剔 is off Sorted Missing Characters. mc_id unchanged (7175). Authority: 武明帥.

The 162 numbered entries are unchanged. Authority: 武明帥.

Rule: a forbidden character is never linked (it has no page and never will). Authority: 武明帥.

- **指小詞, 最高級, 迂回語 coined; grammar-term red links resolved:** `words/指小詞.md` (diminutive; 指+小+詞, ㄐㄧㄜㄙㄛㄙㄚ), `words/最高級.md` (superlative; 最+高+級, ㄐ⼔ㄍㄚㄨㄍㄧㄆ; alias 最高级) and `words/迂回語.md` (periphrasis; 迂+回+語, ㄨㄏㄛㄧ⼄; alias 迂迴語; neologism); readings compositional, 指小詞 and 最高級 attested on Wiktionary as Chinese grammar terms. Rule applied: -法 names the rule or mechanism, -語 the actual form or expression (hence 迂回語, not 迂回法). Authority: 武明帥.

Homophone callouts between 得点 and 特点 removed, and the "no homophones" sentences on 就鳥 and 統率. - **One sound per character: 就 fixed (found that 禿就 breaks the cardinal rule that every character has one and only one sound):** 就 had a second sound, ㄐㄨㄛ, used when it stands in for 鷲. Fixed in four words, 禿就, 就鳥, 海就 and 白頭就: 注音, 諺文 and 羅馬字 now equal the characters' own readings (for example 禿就 is ㄊㄛㄎㄑ⺢ㄧ, 톡쵀, togcwai). The matching rubies were corrected on `Animals` and on the character pages 白, 禿, 鳥, 海, 頭 and 就, and the four 就 rubies in `造化記` (vv. 3, 6 and 9) now read ㄑ⺢ㄧ. Notes rewritten on the four words and on 就 (they had claimed 就's own Notes documented a second reading; they did not). The now-false homophone callouts between 海就 and 海州 were removed. Word and character lints pass. A scan of every word's 注音 against its characters found 11 more words that depart from their characters' sounds: 反乱, 得点, 特点, 杜選, 棕枝, 棕枝主日, 率性, 統率, 瞻妄, 蓬藁, 釩金. - **New neologism 翻飛 and its use in `造化記` v2 ("Let's go with it being a neologism, coin it, and use it"):** 翻飛, "to flutter, hover," for the hovering of *meraḥepet* in Genesis 1:2. A real Chinese word that could not be confirmed in a second language, so tagged `neologism`; the Japanese and Korean fields are character readings. Placed on `Movement` and listed on `List of 新語`. In v2, 揺 is replaced by 翻飛. [AI]

- **`造化記` (Genesis 1), verse 2 reviewed (神霊 on purpose, to appropriate the CJKV religious term; verbs are tenseless; "remove the dot"):** the dot removed from every 於 ruby on the page (4 places, ·ㄛ to ㄛ), so 於 now reads ㄛ throughout. 神霊 kept as the deliberate rendering of *rûaḥ ʾĕlōhîm*. 翻飛 proposed for *meraḥepet*, "hovers"; not applied yet, because 翻飛 is not in the vault and could not be confirmed in a second language (it is Chinese; Jisho has no entry for Japanese). 揺 still stands in v2. [AI]

- **`造化記` (Genesis 1), verse 1 reviewed (太始 for John 1:1 as well, and 創造 for Genesis 1:27):** v27 draft changed from 造 to 創造 in all three lines, matching *bārāʾ* in v1; 作 stays for *ʿāśāh* (v7). John 1:1 in `恵偕記01` already reads 太始於, so it needed no change. The ruby on 於 (ㄛ against ·ㄛ) is still inconsistent on the page and left for a single pass at the end. [AI]

- **New neologisms 業力, 不浄 and 清浄 ("yes, create the neologisms"):** 業力 (the power of sin; Genesis 4:7, Romans 6; attested in Chinese only), 不浄 (*ṭāmēʾ*, ritually unclean; Japanese) and 清浄 (*ṭāhôr*, ritually clean; Japanese, with Chinese qīngjìng meaning "peaceful"). All tagged `neologism`, listed on `List of 新語`, placed on `Sin` and `基督敎`. The 業報 note and the `Sin` 業力-versus-業報 note, and the `基督敎` "not yet coined" line, now point to the words. Still not coined: 冒瀆, which needs 瀆. [AI]

- **Lexipedia `Sin`, 業力 versus 業報 ("add a little spot for 業力 vs 業報"):** a short note in Semantic Range Notes setting the power of sin (業力, not yet a word) against its result (業報). [AI]

- **New word 直霊 ("Create 直霊"):** "upright spirit," a `neologism` from 直 and 霊, taking over the Shinto *naohi*, the spirit that straightens what the crooked spirit bent. It is attested in Japanese only, so it is a compositional coinage and not an attested word; the Mandarin, Cantonese and Korean fields are the readings of the two characters. - **Lexipedia `基督敎`, "Sin in Christian Teaching" ("make a section on christianity and sin detailing these"):** new prose subsection under "Sin, Guilt & Defilement" tying the nine new sin words and the existing ones together: the act and the condition, gravity, against whom, the consequence, defilement versus sin, and the remedy. - **Sin vocabulary, with the Hebrew sense of each ("don't be afraid of co-opting other religions' terms"; "create the terms for sins, and add notes on them about which Hebrew word/sense they best translate"):** 9 new words: 罪業 (ḥēṭ), 業報 (the consequence, ʿāwōn as punishment), 罪悪 (ʿāwōn), 不義 (ʿawlāh), 不敬 (ḥānēf), 背信 (maʿal), 大罪 (a great sin), 罪人 (a sinner) and 天罰 (nĕqāmāh). 罪業, 業報 and 天罰 are Buddhist or Chinese-religious terms taken over under the co-opting principle. Hebrew notes added to nine existing words: 罪 (ʿăvērāh), 邪悪 (resha), 反逆 (peshaʿ), 過失 (shĕgāgāh), 禁忌 (tôʿēvāh), 汚穢 (ṭumʾāh), 禍事 (rāʿāh), 暴力 (ḥāmās) and 有罪 (ʾāshām). New `基督敎` section "Sin, Guilt & Defilement" with a Hebrew-to-Dan'a'yo table; new "Kinds of Sin" section on `Sin`. Not coined: 業力 (the power of sin; Wiktionary has Chinese only), 不浄 and 清浄 (Japanese only confirmed) and 冒瀆 (needs the character 瀆, not in the vault). - **Lexipedia `基督敎`, tribes and patriarchs (Hebrew sounds on the word page, not the Christianity page; coin the tribes, "half-tribe," Isaac, Jacob, Esau and Ishmael):** 19 new words in `words/`. Tribes: 長族 Reuben, 聴族 Simeon, 獅族 Judah, 判族 Dan, 捷族 Naphtali, 武族 Gad, 福族 Asher, 明族 Issachar, 港族 Zebulun, 右族 Benjamin, 附族 Levi, 双族 Joseph, 忘族 Manasseh, 豊族 Ephraim; 半族 half-tribe; 笑生 Isaac, 踵祖 Jacob, 毛父 Esau, 神聴 Ishmael. All but 半族 carry the `neologism` tag. Each name is by meaning plus 族 (etymology where Genesis gives one; Jacob's blessing in Gen 49 or a role where it does not). Traditional spellings are aliases: 聽族, 雙族, 豐族, 神聽. Each word's Notes carries the Hebrew name and its 注音 (e.g. ㄌㄝ·ㄨㄅㄝㄋ for Reuben). 獅族 is a homophone of 氏族 and 踵祖 of 種子; callouts added both ways. [AI]: the Hebrew 注音 forms are built by analogy with the existing prophet and Gospel names; consonant clusters (Isaac ·ㄧㄐㄚㄏㄚㄎ, Naphtali ㄋㄚㄆㄊㄚㄌㄧ) are the least certain. Genesis citations given from memory, not checked against the text.

## 2026-11-05

- **Words for the `Sophomore List`, new coinages and CJKV-strategy fills (coin wherever possible but from existing characters; a word needs two or more of Mandarin, Cantonese, Japanese, Korean and Vietnamese, with Japanese plus Korean alone not enough; "get the idea, not the exact language"):** about 90 new words, each surveyed on Wiktionary, with lexipedia placement and character backlinks. Examples: 貨車, 戦車, 打字机, 昇降机, 歩兵, 騎兵, 砲兵, 爆弾, 洗衣机, 複印机, 猶太教, 印度教, 三角洲, 農場, 旅館, 酒館, 高原, 峡谷, 緑洲, 共産主義, 人権, 独裁者, 紙幣, 公里, 毫升, 疫苗, 抗生素, 維他命, 愛滋病, 中央処理器, 電子郵件, 網站, 下載, 激光, 番茄, 空調, 照相机, 計程車, 加油站, 駕照, 発動机, 能量, 日圓, 美圓, 韓圓, 動画, 葡萄酒, 馬車, 平原, 水道, 稜線, 丘陵, 緑党, 城堡, 磨坊, 神諭, 泉水, 水槽, 網絡, 部落格, 提款机, 投稿, 歩槍, 潜水, 膠片, 閲覧, 空間, 笑話. 洗濯机 was coined and deleted: it is attested only in Japanese and Korean. Function-word gaps (on, for, without, against, next, wish, credit card, windy, reedy) filled with CJKV strategies rather than coinages; `lexipedia/Locatives` gains a paragraph on how Dan'a'yo says them, and `lexipedia/Grammar` says "could" and "would" are not coined by design. Stale "not yet coined" prose rewritten on `Physical`, `Nature`, `Locatives`, `Physics`, `Mind`, `War` and `Tools`. Dots added to several 注音, `kwin` values and homophone callouts fixed to pass the linters (火車, 饗宴, 病院, 水稲, 事態 and the new partners). Not coined: pizza, taco, hamburger, hot dog, DVD, bikini, T-shirt, jeans, addict, and elm and oak as characters of their own (see the next entry).

- **Pound, ounce, portage, elm and oak ("I think 英斤 and 英両 are reasonable #neologism"; "兪木 shall be elm. 楢木 shall be oak"; "X木 is the naming convention for trees going forward"):** new words 英斤 and 英両 on the 英里 pattern, tagged `neologism` and added to `lookup/List of 新語`; new word 陸上, with portage as 陸上運送 (coin 陸上, not the four-character phrase); new words 楢木 (oak) and 兪木 (elm), both `neologism`s, 兪 already carrying 榆 as an alias. 楢 was briefly made a one-character word and then reverted to a regular character whose `stand_in` is 楢木; it is removed from `lookup/List of 名専字` (446 entries). Convention recorded: new tree words are X木.
